SELECT [l].[LeadID], [l].[UserID], CASE
    WHEN [u].[Id] IS NOT NULL THEN COALESCE([u].[FirstName], N'') + N' ' + COALESCE([u].[LastName], N'')
    ELSE N'Unknown'
END AS [UserName], [l].[CampaignID], [c].[CampaignName], [l].[Email], [l].[FullName], [l].[LeadStatus], [l].[LeadScore], [l].[Source], [l].[Notes], [l].[CreatedDate], [l].[LastContactDate], [l].[IsArchived], [l].[ArchivedDate], CASE
    WHEN [u0].[Id] IS NOT NULL THEN COALESCE([u0].[FirstName], N'') + N' ' + COALESCE([u0].[LastName], N'')
    ELSE NULL
END AS [ArchivedByUserName]
FROM [Leads] AS [l]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [l].[UserID] = [u].[Id]
